<div class="home_wrapper">
<div class="home_small_wrapper">
<div class="home_content_block" style="border-top: 9px solid #5d6ae6">
<div class="home_small_block_inner">
<a href="?News=168"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/news/168.jpg" style="min-width: 5%; width: 100%;"/></a>
<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=168">Where on Azeroth is Jenafur?</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_small_content_preview" id="article_168"><b><i>With the Master away, Moroes will play, but who has he invited to Karazhan this time?
<br/></i></b>
<br/>Super, the owner of the Secret Finder's discord, released this announcement earlier in regards to Jenafur:
<br/>
<br/>"Hey everyone,
<br/>
<br/>The following clarifications surrounding the Jenafur secret have been confirmed to the staff team:
<br/>
<br/>- The secret is working as intended, is solvable and does not require a group (confirmed for the current step).
<br/>
<br/>- <b>The first step</b> was to talk to Amara. <b>The second step</b> was the CCL's house. <b>The third step is Kara</b>. It is unknown if going from step 1 -&gt; 3 was a bug, or if it was just designed to find CCL (Crazy Cat Lady, Elwynn Forest) first. CCL does not have anything to do with the solution to the current step.
<br/>
<br/>- The intended solution to go from CCL to Kara was the direction of the 2 kibbles when connected with a line.
<br/>
<br/>I would like to remind people that the staff team do not have any extra inside knowledge and will only post confirmed information from verified sources. While we discourage hints, if one is shared/confirmed with the team then we will post here.
<br/>
<br/>We are happy to take any questions, but the answers will be solely opinions; everything we know is stated above."
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>So, players, it looks like it's back to Karazhan for the intrepid Secret Finder Detectives. 
<br/>
<br/>Will <i>you</i> help puzzle out the mystery surrounding the food found throughout the dungeon? Or will Jenafur continue to elude us all?</div>
</div>
<div class="home_main_content_footer" style="padding-top: 0px"><a class="weblink" href="?News=168" style="text-decoration: none">0 Comments</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content_expand" id="article_expander_168" onclick="news_expand_article('168','200');" style="display: none;">
<center><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/icon_home_down_g.png"/>
</center></div>

                </script>

<div class="home_content_block" style="border-top: 9px solid #5d6ae6">
<div class="home_small_block_inner">
<a href="?News=166"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/news/166.jpg" style="min-width: 5%; width: 100%;"/></a>
<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=166">Meet Rikki -- The New RaF Pet!</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_small_content_preview" id="article_166">On Tuesday, Blizzard implemented a revamp of their <a class="weblink" href="https://news.blizzard.com/en-us/world-of-warcraft/23127426/recruit-a-friend-is-now-live" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Recruit A Friend system</a>, which will now be accessible from your social tab. Along with showing you who you've recruited, it also tracks all the rewards available from the program. For pet collectors out there, it's time to dust off your friend's list and send out a link, because Blizzard has brought us an adorable new companion. 
<br/>
<br/><b>Meet Rikki!</b>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=166"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_181_1" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1002321907.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_181_1"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1002321907.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>His flavor text reads: Rikki has a talent for sneaking in and out of all manner of small spaces, if bribed properly with figs. 
<br/>
<br/><b>Family</b>
<br/>
<br/><ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Beast</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><b>Abilities</b>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Slot One: Swipe / Barrel Toss</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Slot Two: Banana Barrage / Going Bonkers!</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Slot Three: Buried Treasure / For Adventure!</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><b>Stats at 25</b>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Health: 1489</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Power: 259</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Speed: 298</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>My best guess while perusing monkeys is that Rikki is modeled after a type of Capuchin, and he's the reward for the 1st month's subscription of using the program. This means it comes in just above the usual $10 price tag for pets from the in-game shop. 
<br/>
<br/>So how about you? Will you be adding Rikki to your roster?<script>$(document).ready(function() {$('.img_prev_tt').tooltipster({ maxWidth: '800', theme: 'tooltipster'}); });</script></div>

<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=165">Small 8.3 Pet Update</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_small_content_preview" id="article_165">The PTR welcomed another build today with an update, though sadly there’s still no BRD Pet Dungeon in sight, or its rewards. 
<br/>
<br/>However!
<br/>
<br/>We did get to see some fleshing out of a few pets coming in 8.3.
<br/>
<br/><b>Raid</b>
<br/>
<br/>All of the raid pets officially have the bosses they’ll drop from in the Ny’alotha raid!
<br/>
<br/>
<br/><ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Aqir Hivespawn</li></ul>
<br/>This pet will drop from Tek’ris, which is a boss in the fifth encounter and part of The Hivemind along with Ka’zir. 
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Eye of Corruption</li></ul>
<br/>This lovely floating eye of an Old God drops from the Old God of the moment himself: N’Zoth the Corruptor. 
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Muar</li></ul>
<br/>This personal-sized obsidian statue will be dropping from Maut, second boss in the encounter. 
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Ra’kim</li></ul>
<br/>This pet also also slotted as dropping from Tek’ris along with the Aqir Hivespawn.
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Void-Scarred Anubisath</li></ul>
<br/>Not only do we now know this little Anubis will drop from Ra-den the Despoiled, ninth boss in the encounter, but he’s also gotten a bit of a model update. Meet the new model: Now with 67% more swirly void!
<br/>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_173_1" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1140310690.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_173_1"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1140310690.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>Along those lines, all of the Void-Scarred pets have seen a model tweak. They now sit comfortably between the <a class="weblink" href="https://wow-petguide.com/?News=153" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">incredibly void model from the first build</a>, and the <a class="weblink" href="https://wow-petguide.com/?News=158" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">void-light of the last build</a>. No telling if this version will stay, but I have to say it’s an improvement on the other two. 
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_180_2" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1104417550.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_180_2"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1104417550.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>C’Thuffer’s lines look more well-defined, and a little sharper, but it’s a stark reminder that we might need a boar model update sometime soon. 
<br/>
<br/>We’ve also gotten an update on some of the pet abilities:
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Void-Scarred Beetle : Crush / Skitter, Shell Shield / Tranquility, Powerball / Void Crash</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_174_3" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1291121155.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_174_3"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1291121155.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Void-Scarred Cat: Claw / Flank, Bloodfang / Void Slap (New), Rip / Void Cyclone</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_176_4" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1131375443.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_176_4"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1131375443.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Void-Scarred Hare: Scratch / Spirit Claws, Cute as a Button / Early Advantage, Powerball / Void Tremors (New)</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_178_5" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1375156404.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_178_5"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1375156404.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Void-Scarred Rat: Creepy Chomp / Shadow Talon, Vicious Streak / Feed, Survival / Bloodfang</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_179_6" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1025355670.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_179_6"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1025355670.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/> As of right now, it looks like the ability we talked about in the last post—Void Nova—has been dropped from the abilities. We may see it at a later date on a different pet, or they may have just axed the ability altogether. 
<br/>However, there are two new ones: Void Slap &amp; Void Tremors
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_175_7" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1031466116.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_175_7"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1031466116.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div> <div class="news_center"><a href="?News=165"><img class="news_image_small img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_177_8" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1206298644.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_177_8"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1206298644.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>Void Slap may be the replacement for Void Nova, though there’s no way to tell. The abilities do differ in how they distribute AoE damage. 
<br/>
<br/>That’s all for now! We’ll keep scouring each build for more pet updates, and until next time: Happy Battling!

<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=163">Burning Crusade Timewalking</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_small_content_preview" id="article_163">The Bronze Dragonflight is once again calling on the heroes of Azeroth to help repair the timestreams, and prevent catastrophic changes from happening. Of course, that doesn’t mean we can’t collect a few pets along the way!
<br/>
<br/>It may not seem obvious at a glance, but the Burning Crusade timewalking vendor does sell pets, or rather the chance for pets to drop. When you visit Cupri in the middle of Shattrath on the second floor of the Terrace of Light, you’ll see she has Bag of Fishing Treasure for 150 Timewarped Badges. This bag has the chance for four adorable baby crocolisks to drop:
<br/>
<br/><ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Chuck</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Muckbreath</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Snarly</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Toothy</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/>Now, if you’re looking to save your Timewarped Badges for other items, these pets can still be collected in two other ways:  the auction house, and a fishing daily from the Burning Crusade expansion. 
<br/>
<br/>If you also want to keep your gold in pocket, you can park an alt next to Old Man Barlo and keep an eye out for the daily quest: Crocolisks in the City. Old Man Barlo can be found northeast of Shattrath at the Slimyr Lake in Terrokar Forest, at the coordinates 38.6, 12.8 . As far as can be seen, this is the only daily of his five that contains the crocolisks as part of the loot table. 
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>Of course, the timewalking pets don’t stop there! 
<br/>
<br/>The heroic dungeon Magisters’ Terrace also has a chance for the Phoenix Hatchling pet to drop from Kael’thas Sunstrider.
<br/>
<br/>And the Black Temple timewalking raid will also drop pets from all their respective bosses.
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>High Warlord Naj'entus -- Leviathan Hatchling</li></ul>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Supremus -- Abyssius</li></ul>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Reliquary of the Lost -- Fragment of Anger, Fragment of Desire, or the Fragment of Suffering</li></ul>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Mother Shahraz -- Sister of Temptation</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>As a lovely little bonus, all the raid pets count toward the pet collection achievement – Raiding with Leashes III : Drinkin’ from the Sunwell, which rewards the non-battling pet K’ute. 
<br/>
<br/>Every pet listed above—aside from K’ute—is also cageable. So, if you don't want to add a duplicate to your collection you can put them up on your Auction House, or gift them to a friend in need. It’s entirely up to you!
<br/>
<br/>We hope your timewalking adventures are successful, and happy pet battling to you all!
</div>
</div>

<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=133">A few updates!</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_small_content_preview" id="article_133">Hey :)
<br/>
<br/>Thanks everyone for reaching out about volunteer positions! Still looking, so if you're interested, give me a shout on Discord.
<br/>
<br/>Meanwhile, I have a few news to share around Xu-Fu:
<br/>
<br/>1. A warm (re-) Welcome to the first three volunteers: <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="1225" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=1225" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Nyari</a></span>, <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="4" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=4" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Remte</a></span> and <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="3956" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=3956" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">morazor#2212</a></span>!
<br/>
<br/><span class="username tooltipstered" rel="1225" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=1225" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Nyari</a></span> will work mostly as <b>News Writer</b> and <b>Content Creator</b> for the German version of the page and I will help develop some better tools to make localized versions of the page easier to navigate.
<br/><span class="username tooltipstered" rel="4" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=4" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Remte</a></span> has always been very active in posting advice and finding errors on the page and will now be a <b>Strategy Curator</b>.
<br/><span class="username tooltipstered" rel="3956" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=3956" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">morazor#2212</a></span> is the author of <a class="weblink" href="http://magpie.wow-petguide.com" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Magpie</a> and as such the role Content Creator can't be more fitting!
<br/>
<br/>2. You now have the option to include the strategy steps within rematch strings! It is on by default, but you have a slider right below the Rematch button to turn that off, if you wish.
<br/>
<br/>3. A shout out to all strategy creators: I will remove the option to modify tags. Unfortunately, the tags system has been used very inconsistently, and sometimes even to push new strategies to the top by adding every positive tag available without them being true. In the future, more tags will be automated (especially the "Reliable" one), and only strategy curators will have access to tags. In that light, I will also remove all custom tags that have been added so far. 
<br/>Sorry for any confusion or inconvenience because of this!</div>
</div>

<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=131">New view on your strategies</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_small_content_preview" id="article_131">When Legion was released, I struggled very hard to keep up with the world quests and test+add strategies. Now with BfA, since everyone can upload strategies, every one of the new quests has a number of different alternatives available already. 
<br/>
<br/>It worked out very well, all thanks to you, the creators of all those strategies out there. <b>A BIG THANK YOU!</b>
<br/>
<br/>And as a little treat I've just completed the last important feature for it: A "My Strategies" section!
<br/>Hover over your name on the top right, check it out. You'll see a summary of your strats, how they do, and you get notifications if someone writes a comment on your strategy :-)</div>
</div>
<div class="home_main_content_footer" style="padding-top: 0px"><a class="weblink" href="?News=131" style="text-decoration: none">12 Comments</a></div>
<div class="home_main_content_expand" id="article_expander_131" onclick="news_expand_article('131','200');" style="display: none;">

<a href="?News=160"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/news/160.jpg" style="min-width: 5%; width: 100%;"/></a>
<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=160" style="font-size: 22px">Hallow's End 2019</a></div>
<div class="home_main_subtitle subtitle_text">PET NEWS - BY <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="2434" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=2434" target="_blank"><b>DRAGONSAFTERDARK</b></a></span></div><b>
<div class="home_main_date subtitle_text"><span name="time">2019-10-18 21:25:12</span></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_main_content_preview_first" id="article_160">
                        It’s that time of year again! Pumpkins are glowing, fingers are sticky from candy, and the Headless Horseman is setting everything on fire. Hallow’s End went live today, and with it comes the opportunity for all those wonderful holiday pets. Though we haven’t gotten any new ones to add to our roster this year, this is the perfect opportunity to farm for the ones you might be missing, or perhaps to sell on the auction house. 
<br/>
<br/>Hallow’s End boasts a whopping 9 pets that come from a variety of sources. Below, we’ll give you some pictures and details on how to complete your spooky menagerie. 
<br/>
<br/>
<br/><ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Creepy Crate</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_153_1" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1334938363.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_153_1"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1334938363.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: No
<br/>
<br/><b>Breed</b>: H/H
<br/>
<br/>This pet is obtainable only through an 8-part quest chain. 
<br/>
<br/>Horde players will need to speak with Zidormi to revert Tirisfal Glades to before the Battle for Lordaeron. Then, head over to the Ruins of Lordaeron <b>above</b> Undercity and speak with Candace Fenlow. She’s in a tent, tending to her cauldron to the right of the Orphan Matron. 
<br/>
<br/>Alliance players need only stroll out to the front gates of Stormwind to find Gretchen Fenlow, who is also tending a cauldron. 
<br/>
<br/>The starting quest for both factions will be called: A Friend in Need
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Cursed Birman</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_154_2" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1030715314.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_154_2"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1030715314.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: Yes
<br/>
<br/><b>Breed</b>: P/S
<br/>
<br/>This pet can be bought with 150 Tricky Treats from the pet vendors: Woim &amp; Pippi. 
<br/>
<br/>Woim can be found in the Ruins of Lordaeron for Horde players, and Pippi can be found outside the gates of Stormwind for Alliance players. 
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Feline Familiar</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_155_3" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1236349992.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_155_3"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1236349992.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: Yes
<br/>
<br/><b>Breed</b>: P/B
<br/>
<br/>This pet can be bought with 150 Tricky Treats from the pet vendors: Woim &amp; Pippi. 
<br/>
<br/>Woim can be found in the Ruins of Lordaeron for Horde players, and Pippi can be found outside the gates of Stormwind for Alliance players. 
<br/>
<br/><i><b>**The Feline Familiar is a pet that counts toward your Crazy for Cats Achievement!**</b></i>
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Ghastly Rat</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_156_4" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1349306230.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_156_4"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1349306230.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: No
<br/>
<br/><b>Breeds</b>: H/S, P/B, S/B, H/B
<br/>
<br/>For this pet, you’ll need to head out to your garrisons in Lunarfall (Alliance) or Frostwall (Horde). You’ll also need to be at least level 100 and have a level 3 garrison. Once there, you’ll find a goblin vendor named Izzy Hollyfizzle who sells a wide variety of items. 
<br/>
<br/>However, to cage this pet you’ll need to purchase the item Creepy Crawlers for 5 Spooky Supplies. Spooky Supplies are obtained by completing daily quests offered by the orc Orukan, who can be found next to Izzy Hollyfizzle. 
<br/>
<br/>Once you get your five Spooky Supplies, purchase the Creepy Crawlers from Izzy and use the item. It will be consumed on use, and after that you should see pets have now spawned in your garrison for catching!
<br/>
<br/>Another way to do this is to find someone who already has the decorations in their garrison, and see if they’ll let you pop in for a visit and some pet battling!
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Ghost Maggot</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_157_5" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1224510535.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_157_5"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1224510535.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: No
<br/>
<br/><b>Breeds</b>: B/B, S/S, P/S, P/B
<br/>
<br/>For this pet, you’ll need to head out to your garrisons in Lunarfall (Alliance) or Frostwall (Horde). You’ll also need to be at least level 100 and have a level 3 garrison. Once there, you’ll find a goblin vendor named Izzy Hollyfizzle who sells a wide variety of items. 
<br/>
<br/>However, to cage this pet you’ll need to purchase the item Creepy Crawlers for 5 Spooky Supplies. Spooky Supplies are obtained by completing daily quests offered by the orc Orukan, who can be found next to Izzy Hollyfizzle. 
<br/>
<br/>Once you get your five Spooky Supplies, purchase the Creepy Crawlers from Izzy and use the item. It will be consumed on use, and after that you should see pets have now spawned in your garrison for catching!
<br/>
<br/>Another way to do this is to find someone who already has the decorations in in their garrison, and see if they’ll let you pop in for a visit and some pet battling!
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Naxxy</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_158_6" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1300128667.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_158_6"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1300128667.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: Yes
<br/>
<br/><b>Breed</b>: H/H
<br/>
<br/>This pet can be bought with 150 Tricky Treats from the pet vendors: Woim &amp; Pippi. 
<br/>
<br/>Woim can be found in the Ruins of Lordaeron for Horde players, and Pippi can be found outside the gates of Stormwind for Alliance players. 
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Sinister Squashling</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_159_7" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1173184991.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_159_7"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1173184991.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: Yes
<br/>
<br/><b>Breed</b>: P/B
<br/>
<br/>This pet actually has three sources you can obtain it from:
<br/>
<br/>-- The Loot-Filled Pumpkin that spawns for 110-120 level players after the defeat of the Headless Horseman
<br/>
<br/>-- Handful of Treats obtained from Candy Buckets around the world
<br/>
<br/>-- It can also be bought with 150 Tricky Treats from the pet vendors: Woim &amp; Pippi. 
<br/>
<br/>Woim can be found in the Ruins of Lordaeron for Horde players, and Pippi can be found outside the gates of Stormwind for Alliance players.
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Spectral Spinner</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_160_8" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1386044096.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_160_8"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1386044096.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: No
<br/>
<br/><b>Breeds</b>: S/S, P/S, H/S, P/B, S/B
<br/>
<br/>For this pet, you’ll need to head out to your garrisons in Lunarfall (Alliance) or Frostwall (Horde). You’ll also need to be at least level 100 and have a level 3 garrison. Once there, you’ll find a goblin vendor named Izzy Hollyfizzle who sells a wide variety of items. 
<br/>
<br/>However, to cage this pet you’ll need to purchase the item Creepy Crawlers for 5 Spooky Supplies. Spooky Supplies are obtained by completing daily quests offered by the orc Orukan, who can be found next to Izzy Hollyfizzle. 
<br/>
<br/>Once you get your five Spooky Supplies, purchase the Creepy Crawlers from Izzy and use the item. It will be consumed on use, and after that you should see pets have now spawned in your garrison for catching!
<br/>
<br/>Another way to do this is to find someone who already has the decorations in in their garrison, and see if they’ll let you pop in for a visit and some pet battling!
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b>Widget the Departed</b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_161_9" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1243613819.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_161_9"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/14_2434_1243613819.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><b>Cageable</b>: Yes
<br/>
<br/><b>Breed</b>: S/S
<br/>
<br/>This pet can be bought with 150 Tricky Treats from the pet vendors: Woim &amp; Pippi. 
<br/>
<br/>Woim can be found in the Ruins of Lordaeron for Horde players, and Pippi can be found outside the gates of Stormwind for Alliance players. 
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>And that’s it – you’ve gotten all your holiday pets! Congratulations!
<br/>
<br/>We at Xu-Fu’s wish you all the best in your battle pet endeavors, and we hope you have a happy Hallow’s End!

<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=158" style="font-size: 22px">8.3 PTR Update ~~ 16 October 2019</a></div>
<div class="home_main_subtitle subtitle_text">PET NEWS - BY <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="2434" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=2434" target="_blank"><b>DRAGONSAFTERDARK</b></a></span></div><b>
<div class="home_main_date subtitle_text"><span name="time">2019-10-17 07:26:53</span></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_main_content_preview" id="article_158">
                        Those who tried to log in to the PTR today found the servers down, which could only mean one thing: more content! Along with a scad of datamined class changes, auction house tweaks, boss model updates, and much more, we were greeted by some new pets. I’m happy to report that we will be awash in void, tentacles, and eyeballs, with some lovely new models and abilities! 
<br/>
<br/>This content update fleshed out some drops we’ll see in three different areas: Corrupted Zones, Horrific Visions, and the Ny’alotha Raid. The vast majority are coming from the Horrific Visions, so it’s likely we’ll be doing a fair few of them to get all the different spawns. No word yet on what these Corrupted Zones are, but they’re likely some form of assault. Perhaps they’ll be part of a temporary event that will run until the next xpac pre-launch/launch? 
<br/>
<br/>Please keep in mind this is off the PTR, so some of the pets don’t have all their abilities yet, and everything is subject to change.
<br/>
<br/><b>New Pets!</b>
<br/>

<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li><b><i>Ny'alotha Raid</i></b></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_139_12" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1290952559.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_139_12"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1290952559.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>

<br/>
<br/>Two of the pets we <a class="weblink" href="https://wow-petguide.com/?News=153" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">posted</a> about previously—Void-Scarred Pup &amp; Void-Scarred Toad—have seen a change to their model. Whether Blizzard will stick with this new model, or revert to the old one, is yet to be seen. What do you think, pet community? Do you prefer just a little hint of void on the pets, or do you like the full void models?
<br/>
<br/><b>New Abilities!</b>
<br/>
<br/>We’re also getting some new abilities, as well as one only previously used by a Blizzcon pet! The one I’m most excited about is a new weather effect called Toxic Fumes:
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_142_15" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1196606509.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_142_15"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1196606509.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>We’ll see this on the pets Anomalus &amp; Rotbreath. This may not be as big a deal in PvE, but it makes me wonder if we’ll see some new PvP metas more heavily focused on using all poison abilities. What do you think? 
<br/>
<br/>As for the new abilities, so far we’re getting five—Contagion Strike, Jade Barrier, Void Crash, Void Nova &amp; Locust Swarm:
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_143_16" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1017030564.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_143_16"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1017030564.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_144_17" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1008973006.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_144_17"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/13_2434_1008973006.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>Contagion Strike will be on Gloop &amp; Rotbreath, Jade Barrier on the Jade Defender, Void Crash &amp; Void Nova on the Void-Scarred Beetle, and Locust Swarm is on the Aqir Hivespawn. 
<br/>
<br/>However, new might be a reach for most of them: 
<br/>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Jade Barrier is a rename of Prismatic Barrier-type moves</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Void Crash is like Elementium Bolt</li></ul>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Void Nova is your typical AoE</li></ul>
<br/>
<br/>We’re also getting the unique ability Eye Beam from the 2015 Blizzcon Pet, Murkidan, released to the raid pet Corrupted Tentacle. 
<br/>
<br/>Unfortunately, the new pet dungeon hasn’t been released on PTR for testing yet, but as a collector I have to admit I’m excited for some of these new pet models. My personal favorite is the Corrupted Tentacle, though Ra’kim runs a close second. How about you? Do any of these new models make you want to run the new Horrific Visions ad nauseum until you’re ready to rip N’Zoth to shreds? Or do the tentacles just creep you out a bit too much, and you’re looking for something more traditionally cute?
<br/>
<br/>Well, that’s all for now, and hopefully the next build will bring more information on these pets, as well as the new pet dungeon. So until next time: Happy Battling!
<script>$(document).ready(function() {$('.img_prev_tt').tooltipster({ maxWidth: '800', theme: 'tooltipster'}); });</script> </div>
</b></div><b>

<div class="home_content_block" style="border-top: 9px solid #5d6ae6">
<div class="home_main_block_inner">
<a href="?News=153"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/news/153.jpg" style="min-width: 5%; width: 100%;"/></a>
<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=153" style="font-size: 22px">Patch 8.3 PTR &amp; Conclusion to the Shadowy Figures?</a></div>
<div class="home_main_subtitle subtitle_text">PET NEWS - BY <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="2434" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=2434" target="_blank"><b>DRAGONSAFTERDARK</b></a></span></div><b>
<div class="home_main_date subtitle_text"><span name="time">2019-10-15 07:02:29</span></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_main_content_preview" id="article_153">
                        On October 7th, Blizzard announced patch 8.3, the latest content update to Battle for Azeroth. Not only will we be battling an Old God, but we’ll also find out what those Shadowy Figures lurking in our pet dungeons have been up to!
<br/>
<br/><b>New Pet Dungeon!</b>
<br/>
<br/>When players logged into the PTR on the 7th, we were greeted with two new pet battle achievements:
<br/>
<br/><ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Pet Battle Challenge: Blackrock Depths</li></ul>
<br/>
<ul style="margin: 0px;"><li>Shadow Stopper – A meta achievement for the completion of the challenge version of five pet dungeons: Wailing Caverns, Deadmines, Gnomeregan, Stratholme, and the newest pet dungeon in 8.3, Blackrock Depths
<br/></li></ul>
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_120_1" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1185139103.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_120_1"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1185139103.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>At the moment, we don’t know what pets will be rewarded for these achievements. And don’t worry, Kelpfin is likely just a placeholder pet until they add the achievement pets in a later PTR build. 
<br/>
<br/>Our Blackrock Depths vendor has also been added, Burt Macklyn, as well as our currency for the newest dungeon, Shadowy Gem. He isn’t showing what pets he’ll have for sale with the currency, but he does have an Unopened Blackrock Supply Crate for purchase. 
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_121_2" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1255249226.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_121_2"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1255249226.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>Blackrock Depths will be a 9 stage pet dungeon, and the Shadowy Figures have finally revealed their faces and sinister intentions. As we battle our way through the dungeon, will we stop them in time before they wreak mayhem with whatever they have planned?
<br/>
<br/>The Shadow Stopper achievement indicates that this may be the end of the Shadowy Figure storyline we’ve been following, and it leaves most of us wondering: Where will Blizzard take pet battles from here?
<br/>
<br/><b>New Battle Pets!</b>
<br/>
<br/>Even though we haven’t been shown what pets will be rewarded for the achievements or the dungeon, two new dropped pets have been added to the pet journal: Void-Scarred Toad &amp; Void-Scarred Pup.
<br/>
<br/>For those of you brave enough to venture into the Horrific Visions inflicted by N’Zoth on the denizens and cities of Azeroth, you might encounter these void corrupted pets. Currently, they show as being dropped in Vision of N’Zoth – Orgrimmar. 
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_122_3" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1097320301.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_122_3"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1097320301.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>Last, but certainly not least, the World of Warcraft 15th anniversary pet was also added onto the PTR. 
<br/>
<br/>Meet Lil’ Nefarian!
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_123_4" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1343564217.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_123_4"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1343564217.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>This adorable new dragonkin pet has also been given a unique ability: Dragon’s Call
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_124_5" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1095336852.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_124_5"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2434_1095336852.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>
<br/><b>Quick Updates on Patch 8.2.5 Pets:</b>
<br/>
<br/><i><b>Papi:</b></i>
<br/>
<br/>It appears that Blizzard has snuck in some kind of hotfix on the drop of this pet, so you should be seeing this lovely butterfly on your Auction Houses, or hopefully in your loot of the Fresh Jelly from the Stormsong events!
<br/>
<br/>The wave number doesn’t seem to matter, as it’s been seen from both low and high wave events. Some people who have received the pet have also reported they did not use the Butterfly Net from Barry, and/or did not have Exalted with the Hive. No word yet on whether or not the Net will increase your drop chances. 
<br/>
<br/>Whatever change Blizzard has made, it looks like pet enthusiasts who have already gotten their mounts will need to hike—or buzz—back out to Stormsong and run some events. 
<br/>
<br/><i><b>Jenafur:</b></i>
<br/>
<br/>This elusive cat is still proving troublesome for the Secret Finder community. They’ve successfully discovered a couple of steps in the quest chain, but they’ve found even more perplexing clues that have left them stumped. 
<br/>
<br/>Here’s a link to their site, and what they’ve discovered so far:
<br/>
<br/><a class="weblink" href="https://warcraft-secrets.com/guides/jenafur" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Jenafur Secret Finders Guide</a>
<br/>
<br/>If you’d like to try your hand at the Jenafur secret and help out, you can join their Discord. 
<br/>
<br/><b>Just make sure you thoroughly read the Pins in the Jenafur channel!</b>
<br/>
<br/>
<br/>Well, that’s all for now. We’ll keep you updated on all the latest patch 8.3 information as it becomes available, so until then: Happy Battling!
<br/>
<br/>
<br/><i>Burt Macklyn screenshot thanks to Lazey</i>

<a href="?News=146"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/news/news_default.jpg" style="min-width: 5%; width: 100%;"/></a>
<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=146" style="font-size: 22px">Strategy Creation Rules</a></div>
<div class="home_main_subtitle subtitle_text">PAGE UPDATE - BY <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="2" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=2" target="_blank"><b>ARANESH</b></a></span></div><b>
<div class="home_main_date subtitle_text"><span name="time">2019-09-11 12:10:54</span></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_main_content_preview" id="article_146">
                        The possibility for all registered users to upload their own strategies was extremely successful. Since then, Xu-Fu has grown immensely thanks to everyone who contributes. On a much smaller scale, however, we saw a number of strategies that were not up to the standards we set out. When I created Xu-Fu's Pet Guides, my goal was simply to provide my own strategies. That later turned into providing the best strategies for each fight and a good variety for all different levels of battlers.
<br/>We still think that's a good goal to have, but it's a vague one. The problem it created was that we never clearly defined those standards we'd like to see on the page. In the last weeks, the strategy curators and myself have formulated such guidelines and they are now ready to be shared:
<br/>
<br/><a class="weblink" href="https://www.wow-petguide.com/index.php?m=Rules" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Strategy Creation Rules</a>
<br/>
<br/>We think these rules provide a good compromise between the freedom of uploading your own strategy and our efforts to keep the page accessible and helpful to everyone.
<br/>Please have a read through, and leave a comment if you disagree with anything in it.
<br/> </div>
</b></div><b>

<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=145" style="font-size: 22px">Welcome to the team, DragonsAfterDark!</a></div>
<div class="home_main_subtitle subtitle_text">TEAM NEWS - BY <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="2" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=2" target="_blank"><b>ARANESH</b></a></span></div><b>
<div class="home_main_date subtitle_text"><span name="time">2019-08-27 19:59:01</span></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_main_content_preview" id="article_145">
                        I have some great news to share, and no, it's not WoW Classic related, sorry. It is, however, related to someone you have stumbled upon almost guaranteed if you've been using Xu-Fu to look up strategies in the last few months. So pretty much everyone who will read this. I am talking about <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="2434" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=2434" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">DragonsAfterDark</a></span>!
<br/>
<br/><div class="news_center"><a href="?News="><img class="news_image img_prev_tt news_center tooltipstered" data-tooltip-content="#image_tt_147_1" src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2_1141047004.jpg"/></a></div><div style="display: none"><span id="image_tt_147_1"><img src="https://www.wow-petguide.com/images/articles/12_2_1141047004.jpg" style="border-radius: 0px"/></span></div>
<br/>
<br/>She is one of the most active pet battlers here on the page and so far has uploaded more than 700 strategies, many of which are the top picks and most favourited for their specific fight.
<br/>If you want to see more from her besides strategies, head over to her <a class="weblink" href="https://www.twitch.tv/dragonsafterdark" style="font-size: 14px" target="_blank">Twitch channel</a> where you might see some live battles going on. With that amount of experience and knowledge, it is probably not surprising that she's now joining the ranks of Strategy Curators here on Xu-Fu! 
<br/>
<br/>Congrats and thank you so much for the invaluable help you are providing us fellow pet battlers :-)<script>$(document).ready(function() {$('.img_prev_tt').tooltipster({ maxWidth: '800', theme: 'tooltipster'}); });</script> </div>

<div class="home_main_title"><a class="home_main_title" href="?News=143" style="font-size: 22px">Patch 8.2 and Pet Update Issues</a></div>
<div class="home_main_subtitle subtitle_text">PAGE UPDATE - BY <span class="username tooltipstered" rel="2" style="text-decoration: none" value=""><a class="creatorlink" href="?user=2" target="_blank"><b>ARANESH</b></a></span></div><b>
<div class="home_main_date subtitle_text"><span name="time">2019-06-27 11:59:20</span></div>
<div class="home_main_content home_main_content_preview" id="article_143">
                        The patch is live and with it the usual suspects - API problems ^^
<br/>
<br/>Importing or updating your pet collection from the armory currently returns zero pets. This is an issue on Blizzards side and we'll need to wait until it's been addressed.
<br/>In the meanwhile, it's the perfect opportunity to build some better error messages in so you know what's going on when trying to update your collection :-)                    </div>

<div class="comcontentdark com_role_0_medium" id="comcontent_57176">Please stop make the page re-posting comments when reloding the page!
<br/>‌
<br/>Learned this today: posted a comment, came back later to see if there was any feedback, reloaded the page: the page posted a copy of my comment. That was not what I wanted. I just wanted to check if my comment was posted and if there were some responses. I did not want to post a copy of my comment from 5 hours ago. And, a humble guess, I think the majority of the users are not expecting such behavior either.
<br/>
<br/>Thanks, and, as said, I love your (this) site.
<br/>-- 
<br/>Tom</div>

<td align="left" colspan="3" rowspan="2" style="padding-left: 7px" valign="top" width="100%">
<div class="comcontentdark com_role_0_medium" id="comcontent_55593">I think we had a much stricter limit 2 weeks ago and I personally don't think this infobox needs more characters because it's there for SHORT creator comments to strategy basics or pet choices and IMHO not to explain every single RNG situation players can experience.
<br/>
<br/>If you can reduce RNG with breed restrictions... then maybe it's better to add them to the pets and not explain what can go wrong with other breeds. Also, I don't see the need to comment on the level pets because that is something that belongs to your pet team (or Rematch strings if you like to add your specific ones).
<br/>
<br/>Most people come here not to read walls of text, they look for the better-rated strategies that match their pet collections, copy&amp;paste stuff they need to import into their addons and only come back if something goes horribly wrong and then mostly to find help in other peoples' comments who also struggled or to find a different team/strategy: I don't think many people wanna read about stuff that already happened to them, they mostly want to see solutions and it's the best way to have them in a well-optimized strategy&amp;team already.
<br/>
<br/>Be honest, does it help when I struggle with this specific strategy and then come back and find something like "if this is happening you are screwed" or "if this is happening and you want pet XP restart, otherwise you win without rewards for your level pet" in your creator comments? I don't think so.
<br/>
<br/>This is not a basic forum so maybe it's better to continue with keeping things short ;-)</div>
</td>
<td align="left" rowspan="2" valign="top">
</td>

<td align="left" colspan="3" rowspan="2" style="padding-left: 7px" valign="top" width="100%">
<div class="comcontentdark com_role_50_medium" id="comcontent_55845">To me, certain character limits tend to be problematic not because of the length of actual content, but because of formatting tags and embedding taking up a lot of characters. This is something I experience on most of websites, not just this one, but the most aggravating issue over here is linking to user profiiles and other sources of strategies, which could be alleviated with internalisation of link paths and @mentions.<br/><br/>About the tooltips themselves… I don't have any clear conclusions one way or the other. Put an effort into it? It's ignored. Leave it alone to keep it tidy? You come out as oblivious and heartless. <br/>Same goes for strategies, a new one? Ignored on behalf of ones that took the spotlight first. And when it's actually noticed? It seems to outweigh another with 300+ flawless executions which somehow stays in the shadow. Therefore, I'm not sure one can account for every visitor upon creating the environment. :-) <br/>The cycle of (tamer) strife can be cruel, and I believe something needs to be done with it nevertheless – but sadly, enough of the philosophy, for at the moment the engineering of more basic aspects has to come first! </div>

<td align="left" colspan="3" rowspan="2" style="padding-left: 7px" valign="top" width="100%">
<div class="comcontentdark com_role_0_medium" id="comcontent_56052">@Lazey, Part II
<br/>‌
<br/><i>&gt; Also, I don't see the need to comment on the level pets because that is something that belongs to your pet team (or Rematch strings if you like to add your specific ones).</i>
<br/>‌
<br/>You are right here. The Rematch string is part of the Info box, and as such it should be parsed correctly. (The string that contains the correct and breed/damage-specific min health requirements). This should be enough, but, I think there are also folks that aren’t using Rematch. They also should know of the type-specific min health requirements. Unfortunatly the default forms of the page don’t offer any detailed entries for the min health. They only offer one entry (for all damage types), and I always put the worst-case min health there (for pets that are weak against the expected damage type)
<br/>‌
<br/><i>&gt; Most people come here not to read walls of text, […] </i>
<br/>‌
<br/>Well, I think, a “wall of of text” is not defined by the amount of characters. To create a wall of text you need two things: lots of characters, and lots of missing line breaks / paragraphs. I’m completely aware of that, and I’m always trying to section and format my texts in a manner that makes it usable for readers ;) I think the limit of 990 chars is not contributing to avoid walls of text. (It’s easy to build a a wall of text with only 500 chars, but I think you got what I mean…)
<br/>‌
<br/><i>&gt; Be honest, does it help when I struggle with this specific strategy and then come back and find something like "if this is happening you are screwed" or "if this is happening and you want pet XP restart, otherwise you win without rewards for your level pet" in your creator comments? I don't think so. </i>
<br/>‌
<br/>Good question. Me personally, when I use a new strat from somebody else, I always read thru the strat, and in the case I really need to level some pet, I then (likely) exclude strats that are mentioning things like “RNG”, “risk”, etc. But: I’m always happy (when using a new strat) when I see some detailed infos, so that I can <i>estimate</i> what is going to happen. But YMMV.
<br/></div>
</td>
<td align="left" rowspan="2" valign="top">
</td>

<td align="left" colspan="3" rowspan="2" style="padding-left: 7px" valign="top" width="100%">
<div class="comcontentdark com_role_0_medium" id="comcontent_55556">Welcome to battling :)<br/><br/>A lot depends on your roster - what pets you currently have, at what levels, and what breeds. More pets, fully levelled and made Blue, of the right breeds, makes everything easier. Since you haven't linked to a WoW character here, I can't see that, and it's hard to make specific suggestions.<br/><br/>I wrote a forum post here suggesting a development path for a battler. You'll have to decide what stage you're at:<br/><a class="comlink" href="https://eu.battle.net/forums/en/wow/topic/17623632232#post-3" target="_blank">https://eu.battle.net/forums/en/wow/topic/17623632232#post-3</a><br/><br/>The general directions of development people pursue are:<br/><br/>1. Collecting. Some people collect pets with not a lot of regard for their battling capabilities. However, since some pets are achieved only by battling, for example Wicker Wraith and Stunted Direhorn, even collectors need to pay some attantion to battling.<br/><br/>2. Achieving. There are many pet battling achievements in game, and following the natural trail through the Taming [Continent] achievements up to Pandaria, and then on to the Spirit Tamers, Beasts of Fable and the Celestial Tournament was an epic journey for me and for many others. It's well worth doing. The Celestial Tournament is still, IMO, the pinnacle of PvE pet battling. However, WoD and Legion and BfA have added more battling achievements.<br/><br/>3. Roster development. Distinct from collecting, this is finding the right pets for your toolbox in the right breeds and levelling and if necessary making them Rare with stones, so that you have the right specialist pet for any possible challenge.<br/><br/>4. PvP. Only a small minority of pet battlers spend a lot of time in PvP, but those who do really enjoy it.<br/><br/>Apart from Xu-Fu's here, you can read and join resources like the official Blizzard forums for pet battles, WarcraftPets.com website, or the WarcraftPets Discord, where like-minded addic.... er, battlers ... and more than delighted to answer any questions you might have.<br/><br/>Please ask again if you have any more specific questions!</div>
</td>
<td align="left" rowspan="2" valign="top">
</td>
</tr>

<td align="left" colspan="3" rowspan="2" style="padding-left: 7px" valign="top" width="100%">
<div class="comcontentdark com_role_0_medium" id="comcontent_55561">+1 for the Celestial Tourney comment alone.
<br/>
<br/>Only really to expand on Gráinne's comment as regards the 'collecting' and 'roster development' sections - which are, as said 'distinct' although they are intrinsically linked - I would like to point you in the direction of the in-game Achievements &gt; Battle Pets &gt; Collect tab. There are a series of achievements called Raiding With Leashes, pretty much all of which can be completed via the auction house - if you have the gold/inclination - or by completing old raids.
<br/>In this content-starved expansion (and it is, it's making people want to do pet battling, isn't it? XD), it's been some small relief to run (short) old content knowing there's a possibility of a worthwhile reward beyond the vague chance of some 75kG pants dropping.</div>
</td>
<td align="left" rowspan="2" valign="top">
